Rather than centering the system around a single agent or a fixed serial workflow, we allow users to instantiate a full research team from one prompt, with customizable roles, collaborative workflows, real-time monitoring, artifact inspection, and rollback/resume control through a unified dashboard. The platform also supports distinct research modes for exploration, multi-agent discussion, and reproduction, making autonomous research substantially more steerable and laboratory-like in practice. A key practical contribution of Claw AI Lab lies in its Claw-Code Harness, which connects local codebases, datasets, and checkpoints to runnable experiments and feeds execution artifacts back into the research loop. As a result, the harness improves not only execution integration, but also experimental completion and result integrity: experiments are easier to inspect, iterate on, and faithfully transfer into final papers, reducing common failure modes such as partial runs and malformed result reporting. In our internal evaluation on five AI research case studies, using AutoResearchClaw as the baseline, Claw AI Lab is consistently preferred by AI expert judges on idea novelty, experiment completeness, and paper presentation quality. We view Claw AI Lab as an early step toward a new paradigm: autonomous research as usable, interactive, and reliability-aware scientific infrastructure.

</details>


### [111] [Is Capability a Liability? More Capable Language Models Make Worse Forecasts When It Matters Most](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22672)
*Nick Merrill,Jaeho Lee,Ezra Karger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现，在具有超线性增长和体制转换尾部风险的预测问题上（如金融和流行病学），更强大的LLM会产生更差的分位数预测，逆缩放现象出现在合成数据（FBSim）和真实数据（COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场、恶性通胀）中。逆缩放集中在分布的上尾，单阈值指标会掩盖这一问题，建议使用连续无界指标评估LLM预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估通常使用单阈值二元指标，可能掩盖模型在分布尾部预测上的系统性失败。作者旨在揭示并量化在具有超线性增长和尾部风险的时间序列预测中，模型能力与预测准确性之间的反向关系（逆缩放）。

Method: 1) 构建无污染模拟基准FBSim，包含合成SIR流行病预测及其线性对照；2) 在真实数据集（COVID-19、麻疹、房地产市场、恶性通胀）上验证；3) 使用分位数分解定位失败集中于上尾；4) 在Llama-3.1系列内进行族内研究，分离模型规模与后训练的影响；5) 比较单阈值指标与尾部包含性指标的评估结果差异。

Result: 逆缩放现象在合成和真实数据上均出现，集中在上尾分位数；Llama-3.1族内研究显示模型规模和后训练均独立贡献逆缩放；领域知识无法可靠修复校准；单阈值指标下逆缩放消失，甚至反转为正相关，而尾部包含性指标则揭示逆缩放。

Conclusion: 更强大的LLM在预测超线性增长与尾部风险时系统性高估上尾，单阈值指标会掩盖这一缺陷，建议LLM预测评估采用连续无界指标（如分布校准度量）与有界二元阈值指标相结合。

Abstract: We document inverse scaling in LLMs on forecasting problems whose underlying time series exhibit superlinear growth and tail risk of regime change, a structure common in finance and epidemiology. On these tasks, more capable models produce worse distributional forecasts. The pattern appears on ForecastBench-Sim (FBSim), a contamination-free, simulated-world benchmark we release, in forecasting synthetic SIR epidemics with a matched linear control, and replicates in real-world datasets on COVID-19, measles, housing markets, and hyperinflation. A per-quantile decomposition shows the failure concentrates at the upper tail, which more capable models shift upward to track aggressive extrapolations of growth, while the lower tail stays put. A within-family study of Llama-3.1 shows that both model scale and post-training independently contribute to this effect. Domain knowledge does not reliably rescue calibration. This inverse scaling does not appear on single-threshold metrics common in LLM forecasting benchmarks, reversing the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on identical outputs. Single-threshold scoring at conventional cutoffs misses the upper-tail cost; tail-inclusive scoring reverses the sign of the capability--accuracy relationship on the same outputs. We recommend that LLM forecasting evaluations use continuous (and unbounded) measures of accuracy alongside bounded binary threshold metrics.

</details>


### [112] [Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22681)
*Sean Wu,Pan Lu,Yupeng Chen,Jonathan Bragg,Yutaro Yamada,Peter Clark,David Clifton,Philip Torr,James Zou,Junchi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CUSP基准，用于评估AI系统预测科学进展的能力。通过对4,760个科学事件的系统评估，发现当前前沿模型虽然能识别合理的研究方向，但无法可靠预测科学突破是否会发生及何时发生，且表现出系统性过度自信和响应偏差。模型表现存在领域异质性，AI领域的进展可预测性高于生物、化学和物理领域。即使提供额外训练数据知识，也无法弥补与全信息设置之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI系统能否预测科学进展这一核心问题，填补了现有评估框架在时间约束和知识约束方面的空白，旨在检验前沿AI模型是否具备前瞻性科学预测能力。

Method: 构建CUSP基准评估框架，包含可行性评估、机制推理、生成式解决方案设计和时间预测四个维度，在4,760个科学事件上进行系统测试，并引入训练截止时间控制的知识约束条件。

Result: 当前AI模型在科学预测任务上表现有限：无法可靠预测科学突破的实现与否及时机；性能存在显著的领域异质性；过度自信和响应偏差普遍存在；额外知识输入无法弥补预测能力差距；模型更多依赖事后信息而非前瞻性预测。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统作为科学进展预测工具尚不成熟，先验知识无法转化为可靠预测能力，性能提升更多来自事后信息而非前向预测，揭示了AI在科学发现预测中的系统性局限。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domain-dependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation advances. Models also exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Taken together, current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress. Access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable forecasting, and performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

</details>


### [113] [AMEL: Accumulated Message Effects on LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22714)
*Sid-ali Temkit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文研究了大型语言模型在作为自动评估器时，先前对话历史的情感极性会如何影响后续判断（AMEL效应）。通过对75,898次API调用的实验，发现模型判断会向历史对话的主流情感偏移，尤其是在模型原本不确定的项目上。偏差不随上下文长度增长，但存在负向不对称性。即使更强的模型也无法完全消除该偏差。最简单的解决方案是为每个项目提供全新上下文，或在批处理时平衡历史情感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型常被用作自动评估器，但先前对话历史的情感极性可能系统性偏倚后续判断，这种效应尚未被系统研究。理解这种累积消息效应对于保障评估的公正性和可靠性至关重要。

Method: 对11种模型（来自OpenAI、Anthropic、Google及四个开源模型）进行75,898次API调用，将相同测试项目以隔离方式或置于以正面/负面评价为主的历史之后呈现，测量判断偏移量，并分析熵值、上下文长度、情感方向等因素的影响。

Result: 模型判断向历史主流情感偏移（d=-0.17, p<10^-46），该效应集中在高熵（不确定）项目上（d=-0.34）。偏差不随上下文长度增长（5轮与50轮效果相同）。负面历史诱导的偏差是正面的1.62倍。更强模型只能部分缓解（如Haiku -0.22 vs Opus -0.17; Nano -0.34 vs GPT-5.2 -0.17）。token概率分布连续偏移，位置无关。

Conclusion: LLM评估中存在系统性历史情感偏倚，无法通过单纯扩展上下文或使用更强模型完全消除。推荐实践：为每个评估项目使用全新上下文；若必须批处理，应平衡历史中的正负面评价。

Abstract: Large language models are routinely used as automated evaluators: to review code, moderate content, or score outputs, often with many items passing through one conversation. We ask whether the polarity of prior conversation history biases subsequent judgments, an effect we call the accumulated message effect on LLM judgments (AMEL). Across 75,898 API calls to 11 models from 4 providers (OpenAI, Anthropic, Google, and four open-source models), we present identical test items in isolation or following histories saturated with predominantly positive or negative evaluations.
  Models shift toward the conversation's prevailing polarity (d = -0.17, p < 10^-46). The effect concentrates on items where the model is genuinely uncertain at baseline (d = -0.34 for high-entropy items, vs d = -0.15 when the baseline is deterministic). Bias does not grow with context length: 5 prior turns and 50 produce the same shift (Spearman |r| < 0.01; OLS slope p = 0.80). And there is a negativity asymmetry: paired per item, negative histories induce 1.62x more bias than positive (t = 13.46, p < 10^-39, n = 2,481). Scaling helps but does not solve it (Anthropic: Haiku -0.22 to Opus -0.17; OpenAI: Nano -0.34 to GPT-5.2 -0.17).
  Three follow-ups narrow the mechanism. The token probability distribution shifts continuously, not at a threshold. The negativity asymmetry has both token-level and semantic components, though attributing the balance is exploratory at our sample sizes. Position does not matter: five biased turns anywhere in a 50-turn history produce the same shift. The simplest fix for evaluation pipelines is a fresh context per item; when batching is unavoidable, balancing the history helps.

</details>


### [114] [Parametric Modular Answer Set Programs Made Declarative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22716)
*Jorge Fandinno,Yuliya Lierler,Torsten Schaub*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了参数化模块逻辑程序这一新形式体系，用于实现一阶回答集编程中的模块化，能够捕捉clingo-programs的集体控制语义，并建立了模块化ASP的理论基础及其与传统非模块化ASP的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一阶回答集编程（ASP）中的模块化概念，以支持子程序的结构化定义、参数化实例化以及内涵性声明，从而增强ASP的表达能力和可重用性。

Method: 提出了参数化模块逻辑程序（parametric modular logic programs），这是一种新的形式体系，允许定义带参数和内涵性声明的子程序，并通过该形式体系捕捉clingo-programs中集体控制（collective control）特性的语义。

Result: 成功建立了模块化ASP的理论基础，展示了该形式体系能够捕获clingo-programs的集体控制语义，并建立了与传统非模块化ASP之间的理论联系。

Conclusion: 参数化模块逻辑程序为ASP提供了有效的模块化框架，既保持了理论严谨性，又具有实际可用性，为大规模ASP程序的模块化开发奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the concept of modularity in first-order answer set programming (ASP). We introduce a new formalism called parametric modular logic programs, which allows defining subprograms with parameters and intensionality statements. We demonstrate how this formalism can capture the semantics of clingo-programs with collective control, a feature that enables structuring and instantiating subprograms. We provide theoretical foundations for modular ASP, illustrate its usefulness, and connect to traditional non-modular ASP.

</details>


### [115] [Advancing Mathematics Research with AI-Driven Formal Proof Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22763)
*George Tsoukalas,Anton Kovsharov,Sergey Shirobokov,Anja Surina,Moritz Firsching,Gergely Bérczi,Francisco J. R. Ruiz,Arun Suggala,Adam Zsolt Wagner,Eric Wieser,Lei Yu,Aja Huang,Miklós Z. Horváth,Andrew Ferrauiolo,Henryk Michalewski,Codrut Grosu,Thomas Hubert,Matej Balog,Pushmeet Kohli,Swarat Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次大规模评估了使用LLM结合Lean形式化证明系统解决开放数学问题的能力。最先进的智能体以每个问题数百美元的成本，自主解决了353个开放Erdős问题中的9个，证明了492个OEIS猜想中的44个，并正在组合数学、优化、图论、代数几何和量子光学研究中得到应用。基础智能体（交替进行LLM生成与Lean验证）也复现了Erdős的成功，但在最难问题上成本更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理方面日益强大，但其不可靠性限制了在数学研究中的应用。缓解方法之一是使用LLM生成Lean等语言的形式化证明。然而，该方法解决开放问题的能力此前缺乏大规模评估。

Method: 采用两种智能体设计：1）最先进的智能体（具体设计未详述）；2）基础智能体，交替进行LLM-based生成与Lean-based验证。在353个开放Erdős问题和492个OEIS猜想上进行评估。

Result: 最先进智能体：解决9/353 Erdős问题（每个问题成本数百美元），证明44/492 OEIS猜想。基础智能体复现了Erdős成功但在最难问题上成本更高。系统正在多个数学分支的实际研究中使用。

Conclusion: AI辅助的形式化证明搜索在解决开放数学问题方面具有显著能力，不同智能体设计对性能和成本有重要影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly excel at mathematical reasoning, but their unreliability limits their utility in mathematics research. A mitigation is using LLMs to generate formal proofs in languages like Lean. We perform the first large-scale evaluation of this method's ability to solve open problems. Our most capable agent autonomously resolved 9 of 353 open Erdős problems at the per-problem cost of a few hundred dollars, proved 44/492 OEIS conjectures, and is being deployed in combinatorics, optimization, graph theory, algebraic geometry, and quantum optics research. A basic agent alternating LLM-based generation with Lean-based verification replicated the Erdős successes but proved costlier on the hardest problems. These findings demonstrate the power of AI-aided formal proof search and shed light on the agent designs that enable it.

</details>


### [116] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，用于保障基于KV缓存的多智能体LLM系统中的潜在通信安全，通过表示级变换减少敏感信息泄露，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于自然语言的多智能体通信效率低，而基于KV缓存的潜在通信虽更高效但会编码敏感信息（如输入、推理状态、智能体特定信息），形成不透明通道导致隐私泄露风险，亟需安全机制。

Method: 将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在跨智能体传输前学习表示级变换；通过对抗训练框架形式化敏感信息泄露：对抗解码器尝试重建敏感输入，LCGuard学习保留任务语义同时减少可重建信息的变换。

Result: 在多种模型家族和多智能体基准测试中，LCGuard持续降低基于重建的泄露和攻击成功率，同时保持与标准KV共享基线相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard有效保障了KV缓存潜在通信的安全性，为多智能体系统的隐私保护提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [117] [Gated DeltaNet-2: Decoupling Erase and Write in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22791)
*Ali Hatamizadeh,Yejin Choi,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gated DeltaNet-2 通过引入独立的逐通道擦除门和写入门，解耦了线性注意力中记忆擦除与写入的耦合问题，结合自适应遗忘和逐通道衰减，在语言建模、推理和长上下文检索任务上优于现有线性注意力模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性注意力模型（如Delta-rule模型、KDA、Gated DeltaNet）使用单一标量门控同时控制擦除旧内容和写入新内容，导致两者耦合，限制了模型灵活编辑压缩记忆的能力。

Method: 提出Gated DeltaNet-2，引入逐通道擦除门b_t和逐通道写入门w_t来解耦擦除与写入两个角色；推导了快速权重更新视角、融入逐通道衰减的chunkwise WY算法，以及支持高效并行训练的门控感知反向传播。

Result: 在1.3B参数、100B FineWeb-Edu令牌训练下，Gated DeltaNet-2在语言建模、常识推理、检索任务上整体超越Mamba-2、Gated DeltaNet、KDA和Mamba-3变体；在长上下文RULER多键检索任务上优势最显著，且在循环和混合设置下均表现强劲。

Conclusion: 解耦擦除门和写入门是提升线性注意力模型记忆编辑能力和长上下文性能的有效方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: Linear attention replaces the unbounded cache of softmax attention with a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing sequence mixing to linear time and decoding to constant memory. The hard part is not just what to forget, but how to edit this compressed memory without scrambling existing associations. Delta-rule models subtract the current read before writing a new value, and Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) sharpens forgetting with channel-wise decay. But the active edit still uses a single scalar gate to control two different things: how much old content to erase on the key side and how much new content to commit on the value side. We introduce Gated DeltaNet-2, which generalizes both Gated DeltaNet and KDA by inheriting adaptive forgetting and channel-wise decay while addressing their shared limitation, the scalar tie between erasing and writing. Gated Delta Rule-2 separates these roles with a channel-wise erase gate b_t and a channel-wise write gate w_t, reducing to KDA when both gates collapse to the same scalar and to Gated DeltaNet when the decay also collapses. We derive a fast-weight update view, a chunkwise WY algorithm with channel-wise decay absorbed into asymmetric erase factors, and a gate-aware backward pass that preserves efficient parallel training. At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B FineWeb-Edu tokens, Gated DeltaNet-2 achieves the strongest overall results among Mamba-2, Gated DeltaNet, KDA, and Mamba-3 variants across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced on long-context RULER needle-in-a-haystack benchmarks, where it improves the evaluated multi-key retrieval setting and remains strong in both recurrent and hybrid settings. Code is available at https://github.com/NVlabs/GatedDeltaNet-2.

</details>


### [118] [MOSS: Self-Evolution through Source-Level Rewriting in Autonomous Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22794)
*Qianshu Cai,Yonggang Zhang,Xianzhang Jia,Wei Xue,Jun Song,Xinmei Tian,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSS是一个在源代码层面实现自主智能体系统自我进化的框架，通过自动收集生产故障证据、确定性多阶段代码改写、回放验证和容器热替换，在无需人工干预的情况下将OpenClaw四任务平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主智能体系统部署后缺乏学习能力，重复故障只能等待人工更新修复。已有的自进化智能体仅能在文本可变制品（如技能文件、提示配置、内存模式、工作流图）层面进化，但路由、钩子顺序、状态不变性和调度等核心逻辑位于代码层而非文本层，导致一整类结构性故障无法通过文本层修复。源代码级适配是图灵完备的、严格超集于文本可变范围、确定性生效且不因长上下文漂移而退化，因此需要一种在源代码层面实现自我进化的系统。

Method: MOSS采用确定性多阶段流水线：1) 自动批量收集生产故障证据；2) 通过可插拔的外部编码智能体CLI进行代码修改，MOSS保留阶段排序和裁决权；3) 在临时试验工作器中回放故障批次以验证候选修复；4) 通过用户同意门控的就地容器替换进行发布，并配备健康探针门控的回滚机制。

Result: 在OpenClaw基准上，MOSS经过单个进化周期（无需人工干预）将四个任务的平均评分从0.25提升至0.61。

Conclusion: 源代码级自适应进化是比文本层进化更通用的范式，能够触及此前无法修复的结构性故障。MOSS通过确定性流水线、回放验证和安全的容器热替换机制，证明了其在实际生产环境中实现自主进化的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Autonomous agentic systems are largely static after deployment: they do not learn from user interactions, and recurring failures persist until the next human-driven update ships a fix. Self-evolving agents have emerged in response, but all confine evolution to text-mutable artifacts -- skill files, prompt configurations, memory schemas, workflow graphs -- and leave the agent harness untouched. Since routing, hook ordering, state invariants, and dispatch live in code rather than in any text artifact, an entire class of structural failure is physically unreachable from the text layer. We argue that source-level adaptation is a fundamentally more general medium: it is Turing-complete, a strict superset of every text-mutable scope, takes effect deterministically rather than through base-model compliance, and does not erode under long-context drift. We present MOSS, a system that performs self-rewriting at the source level on production agentic substrates. Each evolution is anchored to an automatically curated batch of production-failure evidence and proceeds through a deterministic multi-stage pipeline; code modification is delegated to a pluggable external coding-agent CLI while MOSS retains stage ordering and verdicts. Candidates are verified by replaying the batch against the candidate image in ephemeral trial workers, then promoted via user-consent-gated, in-place container swap with health-probe-gated rollback. On OpenClaw, MOSS lifts a four-task mean grader score from 0.25 to 0.61 in a single cycle without human intervention.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Temporal Contrastive Transformer for Financial Crime Detection: Self-Supervised Sequence Embeddings via Predictive Contrastive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21490)
*Danny Butvinik,Yonit Marcus,Nitzan Tal,Gabrielle Azoulay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出时间对比变换器（TCT），一种自监督对比学习框架，用于从金融交易序列中学习行为嵌入表示。嵌入本身在欺诈检测中达到AUC 0.8644，但与领域工程特征结合时无显著提升（AUC 0.9205 vs 0.9245），表明学习到的表示与现有特征抽象高度重叠。该工作展示了时序表示学习在金融犯罪检测中的潜力，同时揭示了在强领域特征基础上实现增量价值的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少金融犯罪检测中对人工特征工程的依赖，探索通过自监督对比学习自动捕捉交易序列中时序行为模式的有效性。

Method: 提出TCT（Temporal Contrastive Transformer）框架，采用自监督对比学习目标训练Transformer模型，从金融交易序列中生成行为嵌入表示，下游任务使用梯度提升分类器进行评估。

Result: 嵌入单独使用达到AUC 0.8644；与领域工程特征结合后AUC为0.9205，相比纯特征基线（0.9245）无显著提升。表明学习表示与手工特征高度重叠，未带来额外增益。

Conclusion: TCT能够捕捉有意义的时序行为信号，性能接近强特征工程基线，展示了减少特征工程依赖的潜力。但目前在与强领域特征结合时未产生增量价值，尚不具备生产就绪条件，需进一步研究架构、训练目标及集成策略。

Abstract: We introduce the Temporal Contrastive Transformer (TCT), a representation learning framework designed to capture contextual temporal dynamics in sequences of financial transactions. The model is trained using a self-supervised contrastive objective to produce embeddings that encode behavioral patterns over time, with the goal of supporting downstream fraud detection tasks. We evaluate TCT in a realistic setting by using the learned embeddings as input features to a gradient boosting classifier. Experimental results show that embeddings alone achieve meaningful predictive performance (AUC 0.8644), indicating that the model captures non-trivial temporal structure. However, when combined with domain-engineered features, no measurable improvement is observed over the baseline (AUC 0.9205 vs. 0.9245), suggesting that the learned representations largely overlap with existing feature abstractions. These findings position TCT as a promising representation learning approach that captures relevant behavioral signal, while highlighting the challenges of achieving additive value over strong domain features. The results reflect an intermediate stage in the development of temporal representation learning for financial crime detection and motivate further research on model architecture, training objectives, and integration strategies. At this early stage, achieving performance comparable to a strong feature-engineered baseline is itself a meaningful outcome, indicating that learned representations approximate domain-specific features without manual engineering. While not yet production-ready, these results point to a promising direction for reducing reliance on feature engineering in financial crime detection.

</details>


### [120] [Teaching Language Models to Forecast Research Success Through Comparative Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21491)
*Srujan P Mule,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Manasi Patwardhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了语言模型能否在无需实验的情况下预测研究想法的实证成功。通过构建11,488对基于PapersWithCode客观结果的idea数据集，发现小规模模型通过SFT（77.1%）或RLVR（71.35%）可大幅超越GPT-5（61.1%），表明计算高效的小语言模型能成为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型加速科学研究（自动化假设生成与实现），评估和筛选数百个AI生成的想法成为新瓶颈。本文旨在探索是否可以利用语言模型在运行实验之前预测研究想法的实证成功，从而解决这一瓶颈问题。

Method: 构建了11,488对研究想法数据集，基于PapersWithCode的客观实验结果。采用两种训练策略：(1) 监督微调（SFT）将性能提升至77.1%；(2) 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），将评估建模为推理任务，训练模型发现潜在推理路径，达到71.35%准确率并提供可解释性理由。

Result: SFT模型达到77.1%准确率，显著优于GPT-5的61.1%；RLVR模型达到71.35%准确率。离群测试和跨域时间分割测试表明模型对表面启发式具有鲁棒性，并能迁移到独立构建的测试集。

Conclusion: 计算高效的小语言模型可以作为有效的客观验证器，为自主科学发现提供可扩展路径，在预测研究想法实证成功方面表现优于更大规模的通用模型。

Abstract: As language models accelerate scientific research by automating hypothesis generation and implementation, a new bottleneck emerges: evaluating and filtering hundreds of AI-generated ideas without exhaustive experimentation. We ask whether LMs can learn to forecast the empirical success of research ideas before any experiments are run. We study comparative empirical forecasting: given a benchmark-specific research goal and two candidate ideas, predict which will achieve better benchmark performance. We construct a dataset of 11,488 idea pairs grounded in objective outcomes from PapersWithCode. While off-the-shelf 8B-parameter models struggle (30% acc.), SFT dramatically boosts performance to 77.1%, outperforming GPT-5 (61.1%). By framing evaluation as a reasoning task via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), we train models to discover latent reasoning paths, achieving 71.35% acc. with interpretable justifications. Through additional ablations and out-of-distribution tests, we show robustness to surface-level heuristics and transfer to both a cross-domain time-split test set and an independently constructed test set. Our results demonstrate that compute-efficient small language models can serve as effective, objective verifiers, offering a scalable path for autonomous scientific discovery.

</details>


### [121] [Double descent for least-squares interpolation on contaminated data: A simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21494)
*Tino Werner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化线性回归模型中，在训练数据被污染的情况下，双下降现象是否依然存在。结果表明，高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器在强过参数化下会出现双下降，其泛化性能甚至超过鲁棒替代方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在训练数据被污染（存在异常值）的线性回归场景中，过参数化模型是否仍能展现出双下降现象，以及鲁棒统计方法在此情境下的表现。

Method: 在含污染训练数据的线性回归设定下，比较高度非鲁棒的最小二乘插值估计器与多种鲁棒替代方法的泛化性能，分析过参数化程度对泛化误差的影响。

Result: 强过参数化下，最小二乘插值估计器确实表现出双下降现象，其泛化性能超越了鲁棒替代方法。

Conclusion: 过参数化不仅能在干净数据上带来双下降，在数据被污染时也能使非鲁棒估计器获得优异的泛化性能，挑战了传统鲁棒统计的直觉。

Abstract: Overparametrized models can exhibit an excellent generalization performance, although they should be prone to overfitting according to classical statistical theory. The discovery of the "double descent", indicating that the generalization error decreases after a certain model complexity has been reached, opened a new line of research. Robust statistics considers statistical estimation on contaminated data, which, due to assumptions that do not hold on real data, let data points appear as outliers w.r.t. the assumed "ideal" distribution, potentially severely distorting any classical estimator. We address the question whether a double descent phenomenon can be observed in a linear regression setting with contaminated training data. We compare the performance of the highly non-robust least-squares interpolation estimator with several robust alternatives. It turns out that large overparametrization indeed allows for a double descent phenomenon, resulting in a very good generalization performance of the least-squares interpolator, surpassing that of the robust alternatives.

</details>


### [122] [Harnesses for Inference-Time Alignment over Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21516)
*Boyuan Wang,Bochao Li,Minghan Wang,Yuxin Tao,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从推理时轨迹对齐的角度研究LLM智能体的Harness设计，将Harness分解为任务分解与引导执行两种机制，量化了工作流粒度、重试预算和引导重加权对性能的影响，揭示了过分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行等失败模式，并通过实验证明部分指定初始步骤的Harness可能优于完全结构化的工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在推理时使用Harness（任务分解与引导执行）的效果边界，解释为什么更复杂的Harness并不总是带来性能提升，并揭示其失败模式。

Method: 将Harness分解为任务分解（将任务结构化为子目标）和引导执行（重塑局部动作分布）两个机制，通过量化工作流粒度、重试预算和动作重加权来分析性能极限，并结合合成实验与真实终端智能体基准进行验证。

Result: 识别出过分解、过度剪枝和幻觉执行三种失败模式；证明了部分Harness（仅指定初始步骤）在某些情况下可达到比完全结构化工作流更高的通过率。

Conclusion: 有效的Harness设计不必追求完全的分解与引导，适度"不完整"的Harness（如仅指定初始步骤）反而可能获得更好的性能。

Abstract: Harness engineering has emerged as an important inference-time technique for large language model (LLM) agents, aiming to improve long-term performance through task decomposition and guided execution. However, more elaborate harnesses are not uniformly better: increasing decomposition or guidance can sometimes improve execution, but can also reduce final task success. We study harness design through the lens of inference-time trajectory alignment. This perspective separates harness into two mechanisms: task decomposition, which structures a task into sub-goals, and guided execution, which reshapes local action distributions during execution. This decomposition allows us to quantify how workflow granularity, retry budgets, and guidance-induced action reweighting shape the performance limits of harness design. It further reveals concrete failure modes, including over-decomposition, over-pruning, and hallucinated execution. We validate these predictions through controlled synthetic experiments and real terminal agent benchmarks. Inspired by the theory, we further show that effective harnesses can be partial: specifying only the initial steps and leaving the remaining execution to agent can achieve higher pass rate than fully structured workflows.

</details>


### [123] [A Reproducible Log-Driven AutoML Framework for Interpretable Pipeline Optimization in Healthcare Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21528)
*Rui Huang,Lican Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了yvsoucom-iterkit，一个确定性、日志驱动的自动化机器学习框架，将pipeline优化编码为可追踪的日志实体，在Pima和Stroke数据集上分析了超过18,000种pipeline配置，发现搜索空间存在结构化和部分冗余性，识别了关键组件（增强、模型选择、不平衡处理），并通过集成模型实现了稳定且优异的性能（Weighted-F1达0.89-0.94）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于异质性特征、有限样本和严重类别不平衡，准确且可重复的疾病风险预测仍具挑战，现有AutoML方法缺乏可解释性和可复现性。

Method: 提出yvsoucom-iterkit确定性日志驱动框架，将每条pipeline编码为可追踪日志实体，支持组件归因、交互、相似性和跨种子鲁棒性分析；采用Random Forest重要性分析、组件相似性度量及集成策略进行优化。

Result: Pima数据集上增强(0.454)、模型选择(0.198)和不平衡处理(0.101)为关键驱动因素；Stroke数据集上不平衡处理占主导(0.406)。组件相似性分析显示特征选择变体之间RMS距离低至0.0252，mixup与无增强接近(0.0279)。集成模型表现优异（Pima: Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88；Stroke: Weighted-F1 0.94），但Stroke上Macro-F1仅0.67。集成模型变异性(0.023-0.026)低于SVM。

Conclusion: 有效的AutoML优化可聚焦于少量高影响力组件，搜索空间存在显著冗余性，集成策略在性能和鲁棒性之间实现了良好平衡。

Abstract: Accurate and reproducible disease risk prediction remains challenging due to heterogeneous features, limited samples, and severe class imbalance. This study introduces yvsoucom-iterkit, a deterministic and log-driven automated machine learning framework that formulates pipeline optimization as a fully reproducible, configuration-level system. Each pipeline is encoded as a traceable log entity, enabling analysis of component attribution, interactions, similarity, and cross-seed robustness. Experiments on the Pima Indians Diabetes and Stroke datasets across more than 18,000 pipeline configurations reveal a structured and partially redundant search space, where performance is governed by a small subset of interacting components. Random Forest importance analysis identifies augmentation (0.454), model choice (0.198), and imbalance handling (0.101) as key drivers on Pima, while imbalance handling dominates Stroke (0.406). Component similarity analysis shows strong redundancy, with feature selection variants (biMax-biMean) exhibiting low RMS distance (0.0252), mixup closely matching no augmentation (0.0279), and TomekLinks aligning with no imbalance handling (0.0325), whereas Gaussian noise shows greater divergence from no augmentation (0.10). The framework achieves strong and stable performance using ensemble models (Weighted-F1 0.89, Macro-F1 0.88 on Pima; Weighted-F1 0.94 on Stroke), while Macro-F1 remains lower on Stroke (0.67) due to class imbalance. Cross-seed analysis reveals a performance-robustness trade-off, with ensembles showing lower variability (0.023-0.026) than SVM. These results indicate that effective AutoML optimization can focus on a reduced set of high-impact components.

</details>


### [124] [DualOptim+: Bridging Shared and Decoupled Optimizer States for Better Machine Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21539)
*Xuyang Zhong,Qizhang Li,Yiwen Guo,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DualOptim+ 是一种针对大语言模型机器遗忘的优化框架，通过基础状态和增量状态的架构设计，自适应平衡遗忘与保留目标之间的冲突，并提供了8bit量化版本以减少内存开销，实验表明其在多个任务中实现了更好的目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型机器遗忘中遗忘目标与保留目标之间存在梯度方向冲突，导致难以同时兼顾两者性能的问题。

Method: 提出DualOptim+框架，引入基础状态（base state）捕获遗忘和保留目标的共享表示，以及增量状态（delta states）保留各目标特有的残差信息；在此基础上，根据遗忘与保留梯度之间的方向冲突自适应地桥接共享和解耦状态。同时提出8bit量化变体DualOptim+ 8bit降低内存开销。

Result: 在虚构数据遗忘、真实数据遗忘、安全对齐和多任务学习等多种任务上，DualOptim+ 始终在不同目标之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: DualOptim+ 是一种有效的机器遗忘优化框架，能够在遗忘效果与保留性能之间取得更好的平衡，且量化版本能在不牺牲性能的前提下减少内存占用。

Abstract: We propose DualOptim+, a novel optimization framework for improving machine unlearning in large language models. It introduces a base state to capture common representations shared by forgetting and retaining objectives and delta states to preserve objective-specific residuals. This architecture allows the optimizer to adaptively bridge shared and decoupled states based on the directional conflict between forgetting and retaining gradients. We further introduce DualOptim+ 8bit, a quantized variant that reduces memory overhead without compromising performance. Extensive experiments across fictitious and real-world unlearning, safety alignment, and multi-task learning tasks demonstrate that DualOptim+ consistently achieves a superior trade-off between different objectives. Codes are available at https://github.com/CityU-MLO/DualOptimPlus.

</details>


### [125] [Provable Joint Decontamination for Benchmarking Multiple Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21543)
*Zhenlong Liu,Hao Zeng,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出联合包络共形选择（JECS）方法，通过共形p值聚合与自适应BH过程，在多模型基准去污中实现全局污染率可控，实验表明其优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评分方法缺乏理论保证，单模型共形方法会导致模型特定基准，无法实现跨模型公平比较。亟需一种多模型联合的、具有理论保障的基准去污方法。

Method: JECS：计算每个模型的共形p值，按每个数据点的最大值聚合；从右尾观测中重建最大p零分布的保守包络；对包络重缩放后的值应用自适应Benjamini-Hochberg过程，选择具有全局污染率控制的基准。

Result: 在多种模型与基准上的广泛实验表明，JECS在始终维持目标GCR控制的同时，实现了比max-p基线更高的统计功效。

Conclusion: JECS作为首个形式化多模型基准去污的联合共形选择方法，实现了全局污染率可证明控制，为LLM评估的公平性与可靠性提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Benchmark data contamination has become a central challenge in LLM evaluation: when evaluation examples appear in the training data of one or more audited models, reported performance can be inflated and cross-model comparisons become unreliable. A broad line of training-data detection work designs scores to quantify how strongly a model memorizes a given data point, but these score-based methods lack theoretical guarantees. Recent conformal approaches provide provable false-identification control for a single model; however, applying them separately to each model can produce model-specific benchmarks, undermining fair comparison across models. In this work, we formalize multi-model benchmark decontamination as a joint selection problem and propose Joint Envelope Conformal Selection (JECS), a conformal procedure that enables global contamination rate (GCR) control under stated assumptions. Specifically, JECS computes per-model conformal p-values, aggregates them by the per-item maximum, and reconstructs a conservative envelope of the max-p null distribution from right-tail observations above a data-driven threshold. By applying the adaptive Benjamini-Hochberg (BH) procedure to the envelope-rescaled values, we select a benchmark with provable GCR control. Extensive experiments across various models and benchmarks demonstrate that JECS achieves higher power than the max-p baseline while consistently maintaining the target GCR control.

</details>


### [126] [Expectation Consistency Loss: Rethink Confidence Calibration under Covariate Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21552)
*Jinzong Dong,Zhaohui Jiang,Bo Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了协变量偏移下的置信度校准的期望一致性条件（必要条件与充分条件），并基于此设计了无监督域自适应损失（ECL），兼容多种校准方式，具有与ECE相同的样本复杂度，在模拟和真实数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有置信度校准方法假设训练与测试数据独立同分布，在协变量偏移下失效；而现有的协变量偏移校准方法存在类级校准困难、密度比估计不稳定（尤其当比值大或无界时）等问题。

Method: 1) 推导了协变量偏移下置信度校准的必要且充分条件——期望一致性条件；2) 基于该条件提出期望一致性损失（ECL），一种无监督域自适应损失，支持典型校准、类级校准和top-label校准；3) 理论证明了ECL的样本复杂度与ECE相同，并设计可行的迷你批训练方案。

Result: 在模拟和真实协变量偏移数据集上验证了ECL方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 协变量偏移不一定导致未校准的置信度（有更弱的校准条件）；ECL损失在理论上和实践上都有效，且易于训练。

Abstract: Confidence calibration for classification models is vital in safety-critical decision-making scenarios and has received extensive attention. General confidence calibration methods assume training and test data are independent and identically distributed, limiting their effectiveness under covariate shifts. Previous calibration methods under covariate shift struggle with class-wise or canonical calibrations and often rely on unstable importance weighting when density ratios are large or unbounded. Given the above limitations, this paper rethinks confidence calibration under covariate shifts. First, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for confidence calibration under covariate shifts, named Expectation consistency condition, which reveals covariate shifts do not necessarily lead to uncalibrated confidence and provides a weaker condition for confidence calibration than global covariate distribution alignment. Then, utilizing Expectation consistency condition, this paper proposes an unsupervised domain adaptation loss to calibrate confidence of the target domain, named Expectation consistency loss (ECL), which is compatible with canonical calibration, class-wise calibration, and top-label calibration. Third, we prove that computing ECL loss has the same sample complexity as Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and provide a theoretically grounded mini-batch trainable scheme for ECL loss. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of our method on both simulated and real-world covariate shift datasets.

</details>


### [127] [Beyond Single Slot: Joint Optimization for Multi-Slot Guaranteed Display Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21556)
*Zhaoqi Zhang,Jiaming Deng,Miao Xie,Linyou Cai,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang,Siqiang Luo,Gao Cong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多槽位保证展示广告的联合优化分配框架，通过离线二分图匹配、合同轮盘机制和页面浏览约束，解决了槽位冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中等问题。在美团广告平台上的在线A/B测试显示，该方法使ARPU提升28.99%，并显著提高了合同履约稳健性和平台收入效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保证展示广告方法多基于单槽位假设，无法有效优化多槽位页面视图下的广告分配，面临槽位冗余、合同失衡和曝光集中等挑战，亟需一种联合优化的多槽位分配方案。

Method: 将分配问题建模为离线二分图匹配，引入合同轮盘机制处理槽位独占性，结合Page View约束控制曝光量，并设计了可扩展的分配优化算法以实现大规模部署。

Result: 在线A/B测试在70%流量下实现ARPU提升28.99%，DID分析表明合同稳定性显著改善，同时提升了商户ROI、平台收入效率和合同履约稳健性。

Conclusion: 提出的多槽位联合优化框架在实际广告部署中具有强适用性和有效性，能够同时提升平台收入、商户收益和合同履约质量。

Abstract: Guaranteed display advertising is crucial for platform monetization, yet existing methods often operate under a single-slot assumption, limiting their ability to optimize allocation across multi-slot page views. In this paper, we propose a novel joint optimization framework for multi-slot GD allocation, addressing key challenges such as slot-level redundancy, contract imbalance, and exposure concentration. Our approach formulates the allocation as an offline bipartite matching problem with a contract roulette mechanism for slot exclusivity and Page View constraints for impression control, and incorporates a scalable allocation optimization algorithm for efficient large-scale deployment. Extensive online tests on the Meituan advertising platform demonstrate that our method significantly improves merchant ROI, platform revenue efficiency, and contract fulfillment robustness. Specifically, online A/B tests show a 28.99% increase in Average Revenue Per User under 70% traffic, and DID analysis further indicates improved contract stability, demonstrating the strong applicability and effectiveness of our framework in real-world advertising deployments.

</details>


### [128] [From Parameters to Data: A Task-Parameter-Guided Fine-Tuning Pipeline for Efficient LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21558)
*Hao Chen,Qi Zhang,Liyao Li,Zhanming Shen,Wentao Ye,Lirong Gao,Ningtao Wang,Xing Fu,Xiaoyu Shen,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出P2D框架，通过识别并仅更新10%的关键注意力头，在10%数据上训练，实现8.3个百分点的性能提升和7倍端到端加速，验证了参数-数据协同消除冗余的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM领域适配方法中，数据选择与参数高效微调被视为孤立过程，然而实证分析表明二者可能内在耦合。作者希望探索并利用这种耦合关系来降低领域适配的代价。

Method: 提出Strong Map Hypothesis（强映射假设），认为稀疏的注意力头子集在任务适配中起主导作用；基于此构建P2D框架，利用任务敏感注意力头作为双重指南进行样本筛选和结构剪枝，并使用Alignment Efficiency Ratio (AER)度量整体流程成本。

Result: 仅更新10%的注意力头、使用10%的数据，P2D相比强基线获得8.3个百分点的性能提升，并实现7.0倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: 精确的参数-数据同步能够消除冗余，为高效对齐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to specialized domains typically incurs high data and computational overhead. While prior efficiency efforts have largely treated data selection and parameter-efficient fine-tuning as isolated processes, our empirical analysis suggests they may be intrinsically coupled. We posit the Strong Map Hypothesis: a sparse subset of attention heads plays a dominant role in task-specific adaptation, acting as keys that unlock specific data patterns. Building on this observation, we propose From Parameters to Data (P2D), a unified framework that leverages these task-sensitive attention heads as a dual compass for both sample mining and structural pruning. To rigorously quantify the total pipeline cost, we introduce the Alignment Efficiency Ratio (AER) metric for both selection latency and training time. Mechanistically, P2D identifies critical heads via a lightweight proxy and uses them as a functional filter to curate high-affinity data, establishing a synergistic pipeline. Empirically, by updating merely 10% of attention heads on 10% of the data, P2D achieves an 8.3 pp performance gain over strong baselines and delivers a 7.0x end-to-end time speedup. These results validate that precise parameter-data synchronization eliminates redundancy, offering a new paradigm for efficient alignment.

</details>


### [129] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，通过双层次难度评估器（话语级和对话级）和学习调度器，解决多模态情感识别中的模态不对齐与学习不平衡问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上实现了显著且一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话情感识别中，模态不对齐（modality misalignment）和学习不平衡（imbalanced learning）限制了多模态信息的有效利用，现有方法难以充分解决这些问题。

Method: 提出基于自定进度课程学习（SPCL）的即插即用框架，包含：(1) 双层次难度评估器，分别从话语级（细粒度模态特定难度）和对话级（情绪依赖与模态连贯性）评估难度；(2) 学习调度器，根据难度分数动态引导模型从简单样本到困难样本训练。

Result: 在IEMOCAP上，加权F1提升约+1.2%至+6.6%；在MELD上，增益最高达+10.4%，且在不同架构和模态设置下均表现出一致改进。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能有效缓解模态不平衡问题，提升模型鲁棒性，具有良好的效果和泛化能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [130] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测模型中普遍存在的过拟合问题：五个分类器在UCI数据集上均达到AUROC 1.00的完美内部性能，但经外部验证（MIMIC-IV）后AUROC骤降至0.48-0.58，校准误差飙升，共形预测覆盖率从0.80-0.98跌至0.21-0.25，部署就绪度评分均不超过4/16，表明近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移至临床实际。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CKD风险预测模型过度关注判别性能（如AUROC），而严重忽视校准质量与不确定性量化，导致临床部署时概率输出不可靠，缺乏可实际部署的评估框架。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400例，62.5%患病率）上训练逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、Platt缩放SVM和高斯朴素贝叶斯，采用Platt缩放和等压回归进行校准，通过期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准质量，使用分裂共形预测（90%边际覆盖率）量化不确定性，并在MIMIC-IV队列（97例，23.7%患病率）上进行分布压力测试（患病率偏移+特征缺失），最终用八维度部署就绪框架评分。

Result: 所有模型UCI测试集AUROC=1.00，等压校准后内部ECE降至0.000-0.022；MIMIC-IV上AUROC降至0.48-0.58，ECE升至0.68-0.76，共形覆盖率从0.80-0.98降至0.21-0.25；部署就绪度评分均≤4/16。

Conclusion: 近乎完美的内部性能无法迁移至外部数据，任何临床预测模型在部署前必须在外部数据上评估校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [131] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ConTact方法，将抗体CDR设计显式分解为三个级联阶段（学习表面互补指纹、预测CDR-抗原接触、注入接触门控抗原特征），通过距离偏置交叉注意力和接触加权损失函数，在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上实现最优结构质量和表位感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抗体CDR设计方法将"识别哪些CDR位置会接触抗原"和"在这些位置选择氨基酸"两个子问题混为一谈，通过均匀消息传递隐式学习接触推理，导致抗原信号在所有位置被均匀稀释。

Method: 提出ConTact的接触-行动架构：(1) 学习表面互补指纹；(2) 预测CDR-抗原接触；(3) 注入接触门控抗原特征到序列头。包含距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码几何先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失聚焦梯度信号于结合关键位置。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上：结构质量最优（RMSD比次优基线提升7%），表位感知能力最优（F1分数比GNN基线提升10%），序列恢复率具有竞争力（AAR 0.38）。

Conclusion: 显式解耦接触预测与氨基酸选择两个子问题，并通过级联架构和针对性损失函数设计，能有效提升抗体CDR设计的结构质量和表位感知能力。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [132] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出位置加权在线自蒸馏（PW-OPSD）方法，通过诊断发现推理任务中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹级结构（而非局部不确定性），并在多重评测中提升数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线自蒸馏（OPSD）对所有token等权处理，隐式假设教师目标在每条学生轨迹上同等可靠；而基于熵的方法虽尝试放松这一假设，但在推理任务中高熵可能代表有益的解多样性而非不可靠性，缺乏可靠诊断机制。

Method: 提出分支可行性诊断（branch-viability diagnostic）：在学生on-policy前缀基础上强制教师提供的各候选token，检查是否恢复正确答案。基于此诊断发现位置分数是教师token可靠性最强预测因子（AUROC 0.83），进而提出PW-OPSD，在保持OPSD的学生轨迹、教师pass和clip前向KL目标不变的前提下，递增位置权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B上，位置分数AUROC达0.83，局部不确定性最高仅0.57。PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1；在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think上也取得一致的Avg@12改进。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中教师token的可靠性具有轨迹级结构（而非token级不确定性），PW-OPSD能有效利用这一结构且不增加额外教师计算开销。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [133] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，通过自定义模拟器生成合成数据进行训练，解决从Hi-C相互作用图中识别着丝粒等逆向推理问题，能够处理实体数量和大小可变性，并在多种物种中准确恢复着丝粒位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将着丝粒识别等相互作用图推理问题形式化为通用逆向问题，并解决现有方法难以处理实体数量和大小可变性的局限。

Method: 采用Transformer架构处理可变性，并设计定制模拟器生成丰富的合成训练数据。

Result: 在多种基因组大小的物种上准确恢复了着丝粒的基因组位置。

Conclusion: 数据驱动方法能有效利用图谱间的共享结构（如全局对齐局部模式），为Hi-C等相互作用图的逆向推理问题提供通用解决方案。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [134] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EntmaxKV是一个面向α-entmax注意力机制的稀疏解码框架，通过查询感知的页面评分、支持感知的候选选择和稀疏entmax注意力，在KV页面加载前利用稀疏性，实现了在1M上下文长度下最高3.36倍（softmax）和5.43倍（entmax）的加速，同时保持与全缓存entmax接近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，KV缓存内存流量成为主要瓶颈，因为每个生成令牌需要访问随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有稀疏解码方法针对softmax注意力设计，其密集尾部使得任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。而α-entmax能产生精确零值，可将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转化为支持恢复问题，但现有entmax内核未解决自回归解码瓶颈——在稀疏性未知前仍需流式传输完整KV缓存。

Method: EntmaxKV结合了三种关键技术：1) 查询感知的页面评分（query-aware page scoring）；2) 支持感知的候选选择（support-aware candidate selection）；3) 稀疏entmax注意力（sparse entmax attention）。此外，还引入了高斯感知的entmax选择器（Gaussian-aware entmax selector），通过轻量级页面统计估计entmax阈值，自适应调整所选预算以适应分数分布。通过分析截断误差与丢弃概率质量δ的关系，证明输出误差受δ控制，当恢复entmax支持时误差消失。

Result: 在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中，EntmaxKV在匹配的KV预算下，比基于softmax的稀疏解码方法丢弃更少的概率质量、保留更多的支持令牌、实现更低的输出误差。在1M上下文长度下，相比全注意力基线，实现了最高3.36倍（softmax注意力）和5.43倍（entmax注意力）的加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV通过原生利用entmax的稀疏特性，在KV页面加载前实现稀疏解码，有效解决了长上下文解码中的KV缓存内存流量瓶颈问题，在保持高质量输出的同时显著加速推理过程。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [135] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器实际上是在放大预训练模型中的“典型性”轴，而非构建AI与人类文本的边界。通过原始编码器投影即可达到接近或超过微调的性能，该轴在非母语写作者上完全反转，且多种操作验证了该机制的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI文本检测器的工作机制——究竟是学习了一个AI-vs-人类的判别边界，还是仅仅放大了预训练模型中已存在的典型性轴。

Method: 在多种架构（RoBERTa、ELECTRA等）上，比较原始编码器投影、微调、冻结探针、闭式雅可比预测器等方法；通过典型性轴反转预测（非母语ESL写作）进行假说验证；使用三种操作独立的探针验证观测者不变性。

Result: 原始编码器投影在NYT-vs-HC3任务上达到微调性能86-106%（RoBERTa-base上超过微调）；非母语ESL写作上AUROC降至0.06-0.20（反转预测成立）；24样本冻结探针匹配全微调性能；闭式雅可比预测器R²=1.000；多种干预方法在27个单元上校准等价（|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081）。

Conclusion: AI文本检测的核心机制是放大预训练模型中的典型性轴，而非学习新的判别边界；非母语写作者的文本因与典型性轴反向而被误判，揭示了当前检测方法的根本缺陷。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>


### [136] [Value-Gradient Hypothesis of RL for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21654)
*Arip Asadulaev,Daniil Ognev,Karim Salta,Martin Takac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从值梯度视角解释了无评论家强化学习方法（如PPO和GRPO）在LLM后训练中的有效性，证明了actor更新在期望上等价于值梯度，并提出了一个判断RL何时最有效的准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习显著提升了预训练语言模型，但无评论家方法（如PPO和GRPO）为何有效以及何时能带来最大收益仍未被充分理解。

Method: 1) 在可微分轨迹和加性噪声参数化下，证明actor更新在期望上具有值梯度的形式；2) 对离散transformer策略，证明通过注意力的自动微分产生的经验代价态近似值信号，误差由采样间隔和策略熵控制；3) 将RL影响分解为值梯度信号和可达奖励空间。

Result: 无评论家RL方法的actor更新在期望上等价于值梯度，且通过自动微分可近似获得该信号。

Conclusion: 提出了一种判断RL沿预训练轨迹何时最有效的准则，即基于值梯度信号和可达奖励头空间的分解。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. We develop a value-gradient perspective of critic-free RL for LLM post-training. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

</details>


### [137] [Hierarchical Variational Policies for Reward-Guided Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21661)
*Kushagra Pandey,Farrin Marouf Sofian,Jan Niklas Groeneveld,Felix Draxler,Stephan Mandt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层变分框架，将预训练扩散模型的下游适应转化为摊销化随机策略，实现高质量、少步数、低推理成本的奖励对齐样本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在适配下游任务（如逆问题）时，需要昂贵的测试时引导或优化，推理成本高，亟需一种既能保持样本质量又能显著降低推理开销的方法。

Method: 将测试时适应建模为分层变分模型，把控制信号摊销为一个轻量但表达能力强的随机策略；该策略天然支持少步扩散采样（大步长快速推理），并通过结构化逐歩控制维持样本质量；进一步扩展到半摊销模式，结合廉价摊销提议与有限测试时优化。

Result: 在4倍超分辨率任务上，相比最优基线，感知质量更优且推理速度快5倍以上；在半摊销模式下，多个逆问题上达到最先进的感知质量。

Conclusion: 所提框架在质量-速度权衡上表现优异，全摊销模式显著降低计算开销，半摊销模式实现感知质量SOTA，为扩散模型高效下游适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. We propose a principled framework for generating high-quality reward-aligned samples at substantially reduced inference cost. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

</details>


### [138] [I-SAFE: Wasserstein Coherence Metrics for Structural Auditing of Scientific AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21731)
*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 I-SAFE 框架，一种基于 Wasserstein 相干性度量（WCM）的事后分布审计方法，用于评估科学 AI 模型在结构引导扰动下的输出分布相干性，并在药物-靶点相互作用预测任务中揭示准确率无法区分的模型具有显著不同的分布响应特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中常因利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差而表现良好，但这些行为并非基于领域相关结构，导致对模型科学意义的误判。现有准确率评估无法区分此类问题，亟需一种能检测模型是否真正对齐领域结构的方法。

Method: 提出 I-SAFE 框架，核心是 Wasserstein 相干性度量（WCM）。给定训练好的黑盒预测器和外部结构先验，对输入进行结构引导扰动，然后通过三个互补指标评估输出分布相干性：基于分位数的度量（QBM）评估位置级相干性、WCM 评估序数相干性、平移不变 WCM 变体评估形状相干性。

Result: 在 Davis 激酶基准、KLIFS 结合口袋注释和三个序列型 DTI 模型（DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA、TAPB）上的实验表明，尽管这些模型具有可比的预测性能，I-SAFE 揭示了它们显著不同的分布响应特征，这是准确率评估无法发现的差异。

Conclusion: I-SAFE 是模型无关的框架，适用于任何输入可进行结构分解且存在外部先验的领域，能够在准确率之外提供对模型行为更深入的科学有效性评估。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>


### [139] [Correcting Class Imbalance in Prior-Data Fitted Networks for Tabular Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21742)
*Samuel McDowell,Nathan Stromberg,Lalitha Sankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了类别不平衡对Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs)在表格分类任务中性能的影响，并针对PFN的上下文学习特性无法使用基于损失策略的问题，调整了几种经典的不平衡处理方法。实验表明，阈值调整因PFN的校准特性表现极佳，下采样因PFN在有限数据下的优异性能表现相当，且能降低推理计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs在表格分类任务中表现优异，但类别不平衡会导致稀有类别的性能下降。由于PFN的上下文学习动态使得基于损失的策略无法使用，其他技术也未经证实，因此需要探索适合PFNs的类别不平衡缓解方法。

Method: 将几种经典的类别不平衡处理方法（包括阈值调整和下采样等）适配到PFN分类任务中，并在不平衡数据集上进行性能分析。

Result: 阈值调整因PFN良好的校准特性表现极佳；下采样因PFN在有限数据下的出色性能表现相当，且额外降低了推理计算成本。

Conclusion: 针对PFNs的类别不平衡问题，阈值调整和下采样是有效且实用的解决方案，其中下采样还兼具计算效率优势。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>


### [140] [Quantitative coronary calcification analysis for prediction of myocardial ischemia using non-contrast CT calcium scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21745)
*Juhwan Lee,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Hao Wu,Justin N. Kim,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于1,375例患者数据，开发了一种利用非对比CT钙化扫描（CTCS）结合钙组学特征（calcium-omics）的机器学习框架，用于预测心肌缺血。最终模型整合了Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄，实现了98.9%的精确率、79.2%的灵敏度和87.7%的F1分数，钙组学特征显著提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT钙化评分（CTCS）已被广泛认可为心血管风险分层工具，但传统方法主要依赖Agatston评分等有限指标，对心肌缺血的预测能力有限。本研究旨在利用常规CTCS扫描中的定量冠脉钙化评估，开发一种新颖的机器学习框架来预测心肌缺血，以提高风险分层的可及性和准确性。

Method: 回顾性分析1,375例同时进行非对比CTCS和瑞加德松负荷心脏PET心肌灌注成像的患者，提取74个变量（含临床变量、Agatston评分和钙组学特征）。使用XGBoost结合SHAP进行特征选择，通过5折交叉验证训练和评估预测模型。

Result: 在987例患者中，89例（9%）存在心肌缺血。最终模型包含Agatston评分、8个钙组学特征和年龄，精确率为98.9±3.0%，灵敏度为79.2±8.4%，F1分数为87.7±5.3%。钙组学特征显著提升了模型性能（p<0.05）。钙化动脉数量在逻辑回归中与心肌缺血关联最强（OR: 3.63, 95%CI: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001）。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种利用常规非对比CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙组学特征提供了超越传统风险因素和Agatston评分的增量预测价值，有望支持更便捷的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [141] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出文本到优化需要分离建模与绑定两种能力，发现大模型在实例数据增长时存在“有效绑定极限”，并提出BIND方法通过将数值数据外化到结构化文件来提升绑定能力，显著提高准确率且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到优化任务需要两种可分离的能力：建模（选择正确的优化结构）和绑定（将每个系数、索引和参数与具体问题数据对应）。现有模型在实例数据增长时准确性急剧下降，存在有效绑定极限问题。

Method: 构建Text2Opt-Bench基准（12类优化问题），提出BIND推理时方法，将数值数据外化到结构化文件，让模型通过编程方式绑定数据而非从提示中转录。同时微调仅专注于绑定能力的专用模型。

Result: BIND使GPT-5-Nano准确率从59.1%提升至82.4%，匹配pass@5（82.0%）且token成本低于pass@1；GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%。1.5B绑定专用模型可匹敌7B端到端基线模型。

Conclusion: 文本到优化中绑定能力是关键瓶颈，将数值数据外化可有效突破有效绑定极限，绑定能力可独立于建模能力进行训练，且小型专用模型在绑定任务上可超越大型通用模型。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [142] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PEARL框架，通过非参数对比百分位近似建模相对偏好信号，有效缓解推荐系统中用户行为强度不平衡问题，在离线实验和数十亿用户的直播平台在线A/B测试中均显著提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统受用户行为强度不平衡影响——不同用户的交互模式异质性导致观测信号无法真实反映偏好，高度活跃用户信号被过度放大，其他用户被低估，损害推荐质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出非参数对比百分位近似框架PEARL，采用相对优势去偏方法，利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；引入基于预测的bootstrap机制处理稀疏离散反馈，以及广义值加权公式和协同训练策略增强灵活性和表示学习。

Result: 离线实验表明PEARL有效缓解行为偏差，多个排序指标一致提升；在线A/B测试在数十亿用户直播平台实现Watch Duration +2.10%、Consumption Amount +0.80%、Interaction Rate +1.49%、Report Rate -6.91%。

Conclusion: PEARL通过建模相对偏好信号而非绝对交互强度，有效解决了行为强度不平衡问题，理论证明了成对比较可得到百分位偏好信号的无偏估计，在实际大规模推荐系统中取得了显著收益。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [143] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324名患者的数据，利用非对比CT钙化评分图像提取钙质组学和心外膜脂肪组学特征，结合CatBoost-SHAP方法筛选出14个最预测特征，构建了预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病的机器学习模型，实现了83.1%灵敏度、93.8%特异性、85.3%准确率和73.9%F1分数，在零钙化评分患者中仍具良好预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT钙化评分（CTCS）是一种经济有效的影像学手段，广泛用于检测冠状动脉钙化，但传统方法仅依赖钙化评分，未能充分利用CTCS图像中的定量信息（如钙质组学和心外膜脂肪组学）来预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病（CAD），本研究旨在开发一种高级机器学习框架以填补这一空白。

Method: 从1,324名SCOT-HEART临床试验患者的CTCS图像中提取24项临床变量、189个钙质组学特征和211个心外膜脂肪组学特征（共424个候选特征），采用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，最终筛选出14个最预测特征，并利用CatBoost梯度提升方法构建预测模型。

Result: 优化模型实现了灵敏度83.1±4.6%、特异性93.8±1.7%、准确率85.3±2.0%、F1分数73.9±3.3%。结果表明，钙质组学和心外膜脂肪组学数据的纳入显著提升了预测性能，模型在零钙化评分的阻塞性CAD患者中也表现出可靠的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 该创新方法有望改善临床决策，减少对有创或对比增强诊断程序的依赖，尤其适用于低至中等风险患者群体。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

</details>


### [144] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究有限折扣MDP中基于生成模型的递归优化确定性等价(OCE)风险敏感强化学习，给出了OCE下最优价值函数和最优策略学习的PAC样本复杂度，完整刻画了可学习性条件，并建立了紧的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在风险敏感强化学习中，现有工作对OCE这类风险度量族的学习样本复杂度缺乏系统认识，特别是对于递归形式下价值学习和策略学习的PAC可学习性条件尚未明确刻画。

Method: 采用基于生成模型的模型方法，对递归OCE下的价值学习和策略学习进行PAC样本复杂度分析；通过对效用函数u的定义域条件进行精确刻画，区分可学习与不可学习的情形；并建立相应的下界以证明紧性。

Result: 1) 完整刻画了u使得对应OCE目标PAC可学习的条件；2) 当dom(u)≠ℝ时问题不可学习；3) 建立了价值学习和策略学习的紧下界（在状态-动作空间大小SA上）；4) 对CVaR_τ，给出正确的τ依赖为1/τ²，比现有最好结果提升1/τ因子，但在1/(1-γ)依赖上为次优。

Conclusion: 递归OCE风险敏感RL的样本复杂度本质上依赖于效用函数定义域是否完备，不完备时不可学习；CVaR的样本复杂度在τ维度上被改进到1/τ²，但折扣因子维度仍有改进空间。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [145] [Memory-R2: Fair Credit Assignment for Long-Horizon Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21768)
*Sikuan Yan,Ahmed Bahloul,Ercong Nie,Susanna Schwarzmann,Riccardo Trivisonno,Volker Tresp,Yunpu Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-R2框架，通过LoGo-GRPO算法解决多会话记忆增强型LLM智能体中由于记忆状态不同导致的轨迹级奖励不公平问题，结合局部与全局组相对优化、共享参数协同设计及渐进式课程训练，实现长 horizon 记忆的强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练记忆增强型LLM智能体时，多会话环境中不同轨迹因写入、更新或删除不同记忆而处于不同的中间记忆状态，导致轨迹级比较不公平，违背了GRPO等组相对方法的假设，使得轨迹级奖励对长 horizon 记忆操作提供噪声或有偏的信用信号。

Method: 提出LoGo-GRPO算法：全局目标保留端到端的长 horizon 轨迹级奖励学习；局部重播（rerollout）在相同中间记忆状态下比较不同记忆操作结果，实现更公平的组比较。采用共享参数协同学习设计，通过角色特定提示从同一LLM骨干实例化事实提取器和记忆管理器。采用渐进式课程训练，将训练 horizon 从8逐步增加到16再到32个会话。

Result: Memory-R2框架在长 horizon 多会话记忆增强型LLM智能体训练中有效解决了信用分配不公平问题，实现了更稳定和有效的强化学习训练。

Conclusion: Memory-R2为记忆增强型LLM智能体在长 horizon 多会话场景下提供了一种有效的训练范式，其核心贡献在于通过局部重播实现公平组比较，并通过渐进式课程训练稳定多步RL过程。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents enable interactions that extend beyond finite context windows by storing, updating, and reusing information across sessions. However, training such agents with reinforcement learning in multi-session environments is challenging because memory turns the agent's past actions into part of its future environment. Once different rollouts write, update, or delete different memories, they no longer share the same intermediate memory state, making trajectory-level comparisons fundamentally unfair. This violates a key assumption behind group-relative methods such as GRPO, where rollouts are compared as if they were sampled from the same effective environment. Consequently, trajectory-level rewards provide noisy or biased credit signals for long-horizon memory operations.
